Final joint torque equations
============================

Model:
tau = (D(q) + Iact) * ddq + G(q)

Assumptions:
- Coriolis and centrifugal terms are neglected.
- All actuator inertias are equal: Iact = 40 g.cm^2.
- Gravity is expressed in the base frame:
  g0 = [0, 0, -981, 0]^T cm/s^2.
- Centers of mass r_cp are expressed in their local link frames.
- The term U_pi * r_cp is expressed in the base frame because:
  U_pi = d(T_p^0)/d(q_i).

Units:
- Internal length unit: cm
- Internal mass unit: g
- Internal gravity unit: cm/s^2
- Internal inertia unit: g.cm^2
- Final torque unit: N.m
- Conversion used: 1 g.cm^2/s^2 = 1e-7 N.m

Final torque equations:
=======================

Physical note:
If the first joint axis is vertical and gravity is vertical, the gravitational torque around joint 1 should be zero. Any eccentric load on the first joint produces structural bending, not motor torque around the vertical axis.
